1215: {
1216: 	const int32 Magazines = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::SpareMagazines, 0.f)));
1217: 	// A round-by-round gun keeps its reserve in rounds: the same total the magazines would have held.
1218: 	return ReloadsPerRound() ? Magazines * GetMagazineSize() : Magazines;
1219: }
1220: 
1221: void AGBGun::SetAmmo(const FGBAmmoState& InAmmo)
1222: {
1223: 	CancelReload();
1224: 	bFireWhenLoaded = false;
1225: 	Ammo.InMagazine = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.InMagazine, 0, GetMagazineSize());
1226: 	Ammo.SpareMagazines = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.SpareMagazines, 0, GetMaxSpareMagazines());
1227: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1228: }
1229: 
1230: void AGBGun::RefillAmmo()
1231: {
1232: 	CancelReload();
1233: 	bFireWhenLoaded = false;
1234: 	Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
1235: 	Ammo.SpareMagazines = GetMaxSpareMagazines();
1236: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1237: }
1238: 
1239: int32 AGBGun::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
1240: {
1241: 	if (Count <= 0)
1242: 	{
1243: 		return 0;
1244: 	}
1245: 	if (ReloadsPerRound())
1246: 	{
1247: 		// Count magazines' worth of rounds; the result is how many magazines' worth went in, rounded up.
1248: 		const int32 PerMagazine = FMath::Max(1, GetMagazineSize());
1249: 		const int32 AddedRounds = FMath::Clamp(GetMaxSpareMagazines() - Ammo.SpareMagazines, 0, Count * PerMagazine);
1250: 		if (AddedRounds > 0)
1251: 		{
1252: 			Ammo.SpareMagazines += AddedRounds;
1253: 			NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1254: 		}
1255: 		return FMath::DivideAndRoundUp(AddedRounds, PerMagazine);
1256: 	}
1257: 	const int32 Added = FMath::Clamp(GetMaxSpareMagazines() - Ammo.SpareMagazines, 0, Count);
1258: 	if (Added > 0)
1259: 	{
1260: 		Ammo.SpareMagazines += Added;
1261: 		NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1262: 	}
1263: 	return Added;
1264: }
1265: 
1266: // ---- Reload ----
1267: 
1268: void AGBGun::Reload()
1269: {
1270: 	if (bReloading || !bEquipped)
1271: 	{
1272: 		return;
1273: 	}
1274: 	if (Ammo.InMagazine >= GetMagazineSize() || Ammo.SpareMagazines <= 0)
1275: 	{
1276: 		return;
1277: 	}
1278: 	bReloading = true;
1279: 	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
1280: 	ReloadDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, GetStat(GBStat::ReloadTime, 1.f));
1281: 	ResetPump();
1282: 	if (ReloadsPerRound())
1283: 	{
1284: 		PerRound.Begin(Ammo.InMagazine, Ammo.SpareMagazines, GetMagazineSize(), ReloadDuration);
1285: 	}
1286: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1287: }
1288: 
1289: void AGBGun::CancelReload()
1290: {
1291: 	if (!bReloading)
1292: 	{
1293: 		return;
1294: 	}
1295: 	// Blend from the pose the gun is in now (the pinned preview, if any, keeps owning the pose until it is released).
1296: 	if (ReloadPreview < 0.f)
1297: 	{
1298: 		BeginReloadBlend(ReloadBlendSeconds);
1299: 	}
1300: 	bReloading = false;
1301: 	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
1302: 	PerRound.Stop(); // rounds already loaded stay loaded; the round in flight was never taken from the reserve
1303: 	bFireWhenLoaded = false;
1304: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1305: }
1306: 
1307: void AGBGun::FinishPerRoundReload()
1308: {
1309: 	bReloading = false;
1310: 	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
1311: 	// The gun eases back to rest a little slower than a cancel and may still be moving when it is allowed to fire.
1312: 	if (ReloadPreview < 0.f)
1313: 	{
1314: 		BeginReloadBlend(PerRoundExitSeconds);
1315: 	}
1316: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1317: }
1318: 
1319: void AGBGun::FinishReload()
1320: {
1321: 	bReloading = false;
1322: 	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
1323: 	if (Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0)
1324: 	{
1325: 		// The rounds left in the old magazine are lost (provisional, classic tactical style).
1326: 		--Ammo.SpareMagazines;
1327: 		Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
1328: 	}
1329: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1330: }
1331: 
1332: float AGBGun::GetReloadProgress() const
1333: {
1334: 	if (!bReloading)
1335: 	{
1336: 		return 0.f;
1337: 	}
1338: 	if (ReloadsPerRound())
1339: 	{
1340: 		return PerRound.Progress();
1341: 	}
1342: 	return ReloadDuration > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? FMath::Clamp(ReloadElapsed / ReloadDuration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
1343: }
1344: 
1345: // ---- Aim ----
1346: 
1347: void AGBGun::StartSecondary()
1348: {
1349: 	if (bEquipped)
1350: 	{
1351: 		SetAiming(true);
1352: 	}
1353: }
1354: 
1355: void AGBGun::StopSecondary()
1356: {
1357: 	SetAiming(false);
1358: }
1359: 
1360: void AGBGun::SetAiming(bool bInAiming)
1361: {
1362: 	if (bAiming == bInAiming)
1363: 	{
1364: 		return;
1365: 	}
1366: 	bAiming = bInAiming;
1367: 	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner())
1368: 	{
1369: 		Player->SetMoveSpeedScale(bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimMoveSpeedMult, 1.f) : 1.f);
1370: 	}
1371: }
1372: 
1373: void AGBGun::UpdateAimFOV(float DeltaSeconds, bool bInstant)
1374: {
1375: 	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
1376: 	UCameraComponent* Camera = Player ? Player->GetCamera() : nullptr;
1377: 	if (!Camera)
1378: 	{
1379: 		return;
1380: 	}
1381: 
1382: 	const float Target = bAiming ? 1.f : 0.f;
1383: 	if (AimAlpha == Target)
1384: 	{
1385: 		return;
1386: 	}
1387: 	if (AimAlpha <= 0.f)
1388: 	{
1389: 		HipFOV = Camera->FieldOfView;
1390: 	}
1391: 
1392: 	if (bInstant)
1393: 	{
1394: 		AimAlpha = Target;
1395: 	}
1396: 	else
1397: 	{
1398: 		const float AimTime = GetStat(GBWeaponStat::AimTime, 0.15f);
1399: 		const float Step = AimTime > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? DeltaSeconds / AimTime : 1.f;
1400: 		AimAlpha = FMath::Clamp(Target > AimAlpha ? AimAlpha + Step : AimAlpha - Step, 0.f, 1.f);
1401: 	}
1402: 	Camera->SetFieldOfView(FMath::Lerp(HipFOV, GetStat(GBStat::AimFOV, HipFOV), AimAlpha));
1403: }
1404: 
1405: void AGBGun::OnUnequipped()
1406: {
1407: 	CancelReload();
1408: 	bFireWhenLoaded = false;
1409: 	SetAiming(false);
1410: 	UpdateAimFOV(0.f, true);
1411: 	if (bViewModel)
1412: 	{
1413: 		KickElapsed = -1.f;
1414: 		ResetReloadVisuals();
1415: 		HideMuzzleFlash();
1416: 	}
1417: 	Super::OnUnequipped();
1418: }
1419: 
1420: void AGBGun::StartPrimary()
1421: {
1422: 	// Round-by-round gun mid-reload: the press itself interrupts, whatever the fire cooldown says.
1423: 	if (bEquipped && bReloading && ReloadsPerRound())
1424: 	{
1425: 		if (Ammo.InMagazine > 0)
1426: 		{
1427: 			CancelReload(); // the shot then follows through PressTrigger as soon as the fire rules allow
1428: 		}
1429: 		else
1430: 		{
1431: 			bFireWhenLoaded = true; // empty: wait for the first shell
1432: 		}
1433: 	}
1434: 	PressTrigger();
1435: }
1436: 
1437: void AGBGun::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
1438: {
1439: 	if (bReloading && ReloadsPerRound())
1440: 	{
1441: 		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
1442: 		const AGBPlayerCharacter* PlayerRef = GetPlayerOwner();
1443: 		if (bFireWhenLoaded && (!PlayerRef || PlayerRef->IsDead()))
1444: 		{
1445: 			bFireWhenLoaded = false; // a pending shot does not survive death
1446: 		}
1447: 		// With a shot pending the stepping yields after the first round, so a long frame cannot load more shells first.
1448: 		const int32 MaxRounds = bFireWhenLoaded ? 1 : MAX_int32;
1449: 		const int32 Moved = PerRound.Advance(DeltaSeconds, Ammo.InMagazine, Ammo.SpareMagazines, GetMagazineSize(), MaxRounds);
1450: 		if (Moved > 0)
1451: 		{
1452: 			NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1453: 		}
1454: 		if (Moved > 0 && bFireWhenLoaded && Ammo.InMagazine > 0)
1455: 		{
1456: 			// Fire was pressed on an empty gun: the first shell is in, so the reload stops now and the queued shot follows
1457: 			// through the normal trigger path as soon as the fire rules allow.
1458: 			CancelReload();
1459: 			bAttackQueued = true;
1460: 		}
1461: 		else if (!PerRound.bRunning)
1462: 		{
1463: 			FinishPerRoundReload();
1464: 		}
1465: 	}
1466: 	else if (bReloading)
1467: 	{
1468: 		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
1469: 		if (ReloadElapsed >= ReloadDuration)
1470: 		{
1471: 			FinishReload();
1472: 		}
1473: 	}
1474: 	if (bEquipped)
1475: 	{
1476: 		UpdateAimFOV(DeltaSeconds, false);
1477: 	}
1478: 	if (bViewModel)
1479: 	{
1480: 		UpdateViewModel(DeltaSeconds);
1481: 	}

#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"

#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"

UGBStatsComponent::UGBStatsComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

UGBStatsComponent* UGBStatsComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBStatsComponent>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStats(const TMap<FName, float>& InBaseStats)
{
	BaseStats = InBaseStats;
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStat(FName Stat, float Value)
{
	BaseStats.Add(Stat, Value);
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasBaseStat(FName Stat) const
{
	return BaseStats.Contains(Stat);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	const float* Base = BaseStats.Find(Stat);
	return Evaluate(Stat, Base ? *Base : DefaultValue);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::Evaluate(FName Key, float Base) const
{
	return EvaluateKeys(MakeArrayView(&Key, 1), Base);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::EvaluateKeys(TConstArrayView<FName> Keys, float Base) const
{
	TArray<FGBModifierDef, TInlineAllocator<8>> Matching;
	for (const FSourcedModifier& Entry : ActiveModifiers)
	{
		if (Keys.Contains(Entry.Modifier.Stat))
		{
			Matching.Add(Entry.Modifier);
		}
	}
	return ApplyModifiers(Base, Matching);
}

void UGBStatsComponent::AddModifiers(FName Source, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers, float ValueScale)
{
	ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		FSourcedModifier& Entry = ActiveModifiers.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
		Entry.Source = Source;
		Entry.Modifier = Modifier;
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Entry.Modifier.Value = Modifier.Value * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			// Scaled around 1, so a x1.5 buff at half strength is x1.25, not x0.75.
			Entry.Modifier.Value = 1.f + (Modifier.Value - 1.f) * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::MultiplyMin:
			// Same scaling as Multiply: the strongest slow at partial strength stays a slow.
			Entry.Modifier.Value = 1.f + (Modifier.Value - 1.f) * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			break;
		}
	}

	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::RemoveModifiers(FName Source)
{
	if (ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; }) > 0)
	{
		OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasModifierSource(FName Source) const
{
	return ActiveModifiers.ContainsByPredicate([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });
}

float UGBStatsComponent::ApplyModifiers(float Base, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers)
{
	float Sum = Base;
	float Product = 1.f;
	float LowestMin = 1.f;
	bool bHasMin = false;
	const FGBModifierDef* LastOverride = nullptr;

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Sum += Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			Product *= Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::MultiplyMin:
			if (!bHasMin || Modifier.Value < LowestMin)
			{
				LowestMin = Modifier.Value;
				bHasMin = true;
			}
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			LastOverride = &Modifier;
			break;
		}
	}

	return LastOverride ? LastOverride->Value : Sum * Product * (bHasMin ? LowestMin : 1.f);
}
#include "Weapons/GBHeldItem.h"

#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"

AGBHeldItem::AGBHeldItem()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;

	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;

	SetActorEnableCollision(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	WeaponId = Definition.Id;
	Slot = Definition.Slot;
	Element = Definition.Element;
	PlayerOwner = InOwner;
	SetOwner(InOwner);
	SetInstigator(InOwner);
	Random.GenerateNewSeed();
}

const FGBWeaponDefinition* AGBHeldItem::GetDefinition() const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
}

FString AGBHeldItem::GetDisplayName() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : WeaponId.ToString();
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	float Base = DefaultValue;
	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const float* Value = Def->Stats.Find(Stat))
		{
			Base = *Value;
		}
	}

	if (const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats())
	{
		TArray<FName, TInlineAllocator<3>> Keys;
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponKey(WeaponId, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponSlotKey(Slot, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponAllKey(Stat));
		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Base);
	}
	return Base;
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnEquipped()
{
	bEquipped = true;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnUnequipped()
{
	bEquipped = false;
	bTriggerHeld = false;
	bAttackQueued = false;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
}

EGBHudSkin AGBHeldItem::GetHudSkin() const
{
	return Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun ? EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetTimeSinceLastAttack() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? static_cast<float>(World->GetTimeSeconds() - LastAttackTime) : 1000.f;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAutomatic() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return Def && Def->bAutomatic;
}

UGBStatsComponent* AGBHeldItem::GetOwnerStats() const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	return Player ? Player->GetStats() : nullptr;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::GetActiveEnchant(EGBElement& OutElement, FName& OutOnHitEffect) const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player ? Player->GetStatusEffects() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Effects || !Data)
	{
		return false;
	}

	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Active : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
	{
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Def = Data->FindStatusEffect(Active.EffectId);
		if (!Def)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FString* ElementName = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponElement);
		if (!ElementName || ElementName->IsEmpty())
		{
			continue;
		}
		const int64 Value = StaticEnum<EGBElement>()->GetValueByNameString(*ElementName);
		if (Value == INDEX_NONE)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Status effect '%s': unknown WeaponElement '%s'"), *Active.EffectId.ToString(), **ElementName);
			continue;
		}
		OutElement = static_cast<EGBElement>(Value);
		const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponOnHitEffect);
		OutOnHitEffect = (Effect && !Effect->IsEmpty()) ? FName(**Effect) : NAME_None;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

FGBDamageInfo AGBHeldItem::MakeDamageInfo() const
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = GetStat(GBStat::Damage);
	Info.Element = Element;
	Info.Instigator = GetPlayerOwner();
	Info.Causer = const_cast<AGBHeldItem*>(this);
	Info.SourceId = WeaponId;

	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
		{
			if (!Effect->IsEmpty())
			{
				Info.OnHitEffect = FName(**Effect);
			}
		}
	}

	// The enchant's damage multiplier arrives by itself through its "Weapon.Gun.Damage" modifier.
	bool bElementFromEnchant = false;
	EGBElement EnchantElement = EGBElement::Physical;
	FName EnchantEffect = NAME_None;
	if (AcceptsEnchant() && GetActiveEnchant(EnchantElement, EnchantEffect))
	{
		Info.Element = EnchantElement;
		if (!EnchantEffect.IsNone())
		{
			Info.OnHitEffect = EnchantEffect;
		}
		bElementFromEnchant = true;
	}

	const int32 ElementIndex = GBElemental::ElementIndex(Info.Element);
	if (ElementIndex >= 0)
	{
		Info.BuildUp = GetStat(GBElemental::WeaponBuildUpKey(ElementIndex), 0.f);
		if (bElementFromEnchant)
		{
			Info.BuildUp *= GetStat(GBStat::EnchantBuildUpMult, 1.f);
		}
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float DefaultMult = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBStat::WeakPointBuildUpMult, 1.f) : 1.f;
		Info.WeakPointBuildUpMult = GetStat(GBStat::WeakPointBuildUpMult, DefaultMult);
	}
	Info.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
	return Info;
}

void AGBHeldItem::PressTrigger()
{
	bTriggerHeld = true;
	bAttackQueued = true;
	UpdateTrigger();
}

void AGBHeldItem::ReleaseTrigger()
{
	bTriggerHeld = false;
	bAttackQueued = false;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAttackReady() const
{
	return GetTimeSinceLastAttack() >= GetStat(GBStat::FireInterval, 0.f);
}

void AGBHeldItem::UpdateTrigger()
{
	if (!bEquipped || !(bAttackQueued || (bTriggerHeld && IsAutomatic())))
	{
		return;
	}
	if (!IsAttackReady())
	{
		return; // a pressed shot stays queued until the fire interval has passed
	}

	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player ? Player->GetSpellbook() : nullptr;
	if (!Player || Player->IsDead() || (Spellbook && Spellbook->IsBusy()))
	{
		bAttackQueued = false;
		return;
	}

	if (PerformAttack())
	{
		if (const UWorld* World = GetWorld())
		{
			LastAttackTime = World->GetTimeSeconds();
		}
	}
	bAttackQueued = false;
}

void AGBHeldItem::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	UpdateTrigger();
}

FVector AGBHeldItem::FindAimPoint(float Range) const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (!Player)
	{
		return GetActorLocation();
	}
	const FVector Start = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector End = Start + Player->GetAimDirection() * Range;

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBAimPoint), false, Player);
	Params.AddIgnoredActor(this);
	FHitResult Hit;
	if (GetWorld() && GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Start, End, GBWeapon::AimTraceChannel, Params))
	{
		return Hit.ImpactPoint;
	}
	return End;
}

void AGBHeldItem::NotifyEquipmentChanged() const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player ? Player->GetEquipment() : nullptr)
	{
		Equipment->OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

// balance_model.js - reads Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json and prints the balance tables.
// Usage (from the repo root):  node Tools/Balance/balance_model.js
// It changes nothing: it is the calculator behind the numbers in Design/GDD.md section 19.
// Every assumption of the model is in the ASSUME and BUILDS blocks below; everything else comes from the data.
'use strict';
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const dataDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'Glyphbound', 'Content', 'Data');
const load = (name) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dataDir, name), 'utf8'));

// ---- Model assumptions (not game data) ----
const ASSUME = {
	Accuracy: 0.7,        // share of shots that hit (default; per weapon below)
	WeaponAccuracy: { Rifle: 0.6, Shotgun: 0.75, Pistol: 0.75 }, // automatic fire and recoil miss more
	Uptime: 0.7,          // share of the game time spent attacking (the rest: dodging, switching, looking for targets)
	HeadShare: 0.25,      // share of the hits that land on the head / weak point
	// Where a gun's misses come from (shares of its misses; the rest is moving targets and aim). Recoil perks shrink the
	// first part, spread perks the second, in proportion to the stat. Provisional (architect, Nov 13), to weigh the handling perks.
	MissFromRecoil: { Pistol: 0.3, Rifle: 0.5, Shotgun: 0.15 },
	MissFromSpread: { Pistol: 0.15, Rifle: 0.2, Shotgun: 0.5 },
	QteScore: 0.75,       // typical QTE score; the game maps 0 -> Min and 1 -> Max in a straight line (Base = QTE off)
	QteRealSeconds: 2,    // real seconds a player spends in one QTE (the limit is QTE.TimeLimit)
	SpellTargets: 1.8,    // enemies an average spell hits in a wave (1 against a boss)
	AvoidMelee: 0.85,     // share of melee damage a moving player avoids (the player is much faster than most enemies)
	AvoidRanged: 0.6,     // share of enemy shots a moving player dodges
	WaveOverhead: 6,      // game seconds lost per sub-wave: spawn delay, enemies walking in, finding targets
	SkillPointBudget: 36, // points the player should own before the final boss
	Targets: { Wave: [60, 90], SubBoss: [40, 60], Valkyrie: [105, 135], Entity: [160, 200], Opening: [15, 40] } // real seconds
};

// Reference builds: what a player who mixes both trees owns at each point of the game (skill id -> rank).
// Since the per-gun weapon trees (GDD v0.40) the builds are pistol builds; the gun specialist adds the shotgun.

// Which build an encounter is fought with (by id prefix, first match wins).
const STAGE = [['Opening', 'Start'], ['Test_', 'Mid'], ['Wave_Past_1', 'Early'], ['Wave_Future_1', 'Early'], ['Wave_Past_2', 'Mid'], ['Wave_Future_2', 'Mid'],
	['SubBoss', 'Mid'], ['Zone_', 'Mid'], ['Wave_', 'ValkyriePast'], ['Valkyrie_Past', 'ValkyriePast'], ['Valkyrie_Future', 'ValkyrieFuture'], ['Entity', 'Entity']];

const pad = (s, n) => String(s).padEnd(n);
const num = (v, d = 1) => (Math.round(v * Math.pow(10, d)) / Math.pow(10, d)).toString();
const line = (cols, widths) => console.log(cols.map((c, i) => pad(c, widths[i])).join(' '));
const title = (t) => console.log('\n=== ' + t + ' ===');
const skillById = {};
for (const s of skills) { skillById[s.Id] = s; }
const spellById = {};
for (const s of spells) { spellById[s.Id] = s; }
const enemyById = {};
for (const e of enemies) { enemyById[e.Id] = e; }

// ---- Modifiers: (base + adds) x product of multipliers; a perk's Multiply stacks in a straight line with its rank ----
function applyMods(base, keys, ranks) {
	let add = 0;
	let mult = 1;
	for (const id of Object.keys(ranks || {})) {
		const node = skillById[id];
		if (!node) { throw new Error('unknown skill ' + id); }
		const rank = Math.min(ranks[id], node.MaxRank);
		for (const m of node.ModifiersPerRank) {
			if (keys.indexOf(m.Stat) < 0) { continue; }
			if (m.Op === 'Add') { add += m.Value * rank; }
			else if (m.Op === 'Multiply') { mult *= 1 + (m.Value - 1) * rank; }
		}
	}
	return (base + add) * mult;
}
function buildCost(ranks) {
	return Object.keys(ranks).reduce((a, id) => a + skillById[id].Cost * ranks[id], 0);
}
const qteParam = (p) => p.ScaledBy === 'None' ? p.Base : p.Min + (p.Max - p.Min) * ASSUME.QteScore;

// ---- Weapon enchant at a typical QTE ----
const enchantSpell = spellById[player.EnchantSpell];
const enchantEffect = effects.find((f) => f.Id.indexOf('Enchant_') === 0);
const enchantBonus = enchantEffect ? enchantEffect.Modifiers[0].Value - 1 : 0;
function enchant(ranks) {
	if (!enchantSpell) { return { mult: 1, uptime: 0 }; }
	const p = enchantSpell.Params;
	const cooldown = applyMods(p.Cooldown.Base, ['Spell.All.Cooldown', 'Spell.WeaponEnchant.Cooldown'], ranks);
	const uptime = Math.min(1, qteParam(p.Duration) / cooldown);
	return { mult: 1 + enchantBonus * qteParam(p.EffectStrength), uptime, cooldown };
}

// ---- Weapons ----
function weaponRow(w, ranks, useEnchant) {
	const stat = (name) => applyMods(w.Stats[name] === undefined ? (name.indexOf('Mult') >= 0 ? 1 : 0) : w.Stats[name],
		['Weapon.All.' + name, 'Weapon.' + w.Slot + '.' + name, 'Weapon.' + w.Id + '.' + name], ranks);
	const pellets = Math.max(1, Math.round(stat('Pellets'))) || 1; // perks can add pellets (the game reads it through GetStat)
	const near = w.Stats.FalloffNearMult === undefined ? 1 : stat('FalloffNearMult');
	const far = w.Stats.FalloffFarMult === undefined ? 1 : stat('FalloffFarMult');
	const best = Math.max(near, far);
	const hit = stat('Damage') * pellets * best;
	const interval = stat('FireInterval');
	const burst = hit / interval;
	let sustained = burst;
	let rounds = Infinity;
	const mag = w.Stats.MagazineSize ? Math.round(stat('MagazineSize')) : 0; // perks can enlarge it
	if (mag) {
		sustained = hit * mag / (mag * interval + stat('ReloadTime'));
		rounds = mag * (1 + (w.Stats.SpareMagazines || 0));
	}
	const head = stat('HeadshotMult') || 1;
	const baseAccuracy = ASSUME.WeaponAccuracy[w.Id] || ASSUME.Accuracy;
	const ratio = (name) => (w.Stats[name] ? stat(name) / w.Stats[name] : 1);
	const missShrink = (ASSUME.MissFromRecoil[w.Id] || 0) * (1 - ratio('RecoilPitch')) + (ASSUME.MissFromSpread[w.Id] || 0) * (1 - ratio('Spread'));
	const accuracy = 1 - (1 - baseAccuracy) * (1 - missShrink);
	const headFactor = 1 + ASSUME.HeadShare * (head - 1);
	const en = useEnchant && w.Slot === 'Gun' ? enchant(ranks) : { mult: 1, uptime: 0 };
	const enchantFactor = 1 + en.uptime * (en.mult - 1);
	const perShot = hit * accuracy * headFactor * enchantFactor; // expected damage of one trigger pull
	const buildUp = Math.max(w.Stats.FireBuildUp || 0, w.Stats.LightningBuildUp || 0, w.Stats.IceBuildUp || 0);
	return {
		id: w.Id, slot: w.Slot, hit, burst, sustained, head, accuracy, mag, perShot,
		nearSustained: sustained * near / best, farSustained: sustained * far / best,
		expected: sustained * accuracy * ASSUME.Uptime * headFactor * enchantFactor,
		carried: rounds === Infinity ? Infinity : rounds * hit,          // paper damage carried
		carriedExpected: rounds === Infinity ? Infinity : rounds * perShot, // damage it really deals before running dry
		magExpected: mag * perShot,
		buildUpPerSecond: buildUp / interval
	};
}
const baseRows = weapons.map((w) => weaponRow(w, {}, false));

title('Weapons (at their best range, no perks, no enchant)');

}

437: void AGBPlayerCharacter::StartViewShake(float Duration, float AmplitudeDeg, float Frequency)
438: {
439: 	if (!Camera)
440: 	{
441: 		return;
442: 	}
443: 	if (Duration <= 0.f || AmplitudeDeg <= 0.f)
444: 	{
445: 		ViewShakeTimeLeft = 0.f;
446: 		ViewShakeDuration = 0.f;
447: 		ViewShakeAmplitudeDeg = 0.f;
448: 		Camera->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator::ZeroRotator);
449: 		return;
450: 	}
451: 	if (ViewShakeTimeLeft > 0.f)
452: 	{
453: 		ViewShakeAmplitudeDeg = FMath::Max(ViewShakeAmplitudeDeg, AmplitudeDeg);
454: 	}
455: 	else
456: 	{
457: 		ViewShakeAmplitudeDeg = AmplitudeDeg;
458: 	}
459: 	ViewShakeDuration = Duration;
460: 	ViewShakeTimeLeft = Duration;
461: 	ViewShakeFrequency = FMath::Max(0.f, Frequency);
462: }
463: 
464: void AGBPlayerCharacter::TickViewShake(float DeltaSeconds)
465: {
466: 	if (ViewShakeTimeLeft <= 0.f || !Camera)
467: 	{
468: 		return;
469: 	}
470: 	ViewShakeTimeLeft -= DeltaSeconds;
471: 	if (ViewShakeTimeLeft <= 0.f || ViewShakeDuration <= 0.f)
472: 	{
473: 		ViewShakeTimeLeft = 0.f;
474: 		ViewShakeDuration = 0.f;
475: 		ViewShakeAmplitudeDeg = 0.f;
476: 		if (Camera)
477: 		{
478: 			Camera->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator::ZeroRotator);
479: 		}
480: 		return;
481: 	}
482: 	const float Elapsed = ViewShakeDuration - ViewShakeTimeLeft;
483: 	const float Fade = FMath::Clamp(ViewShakeTimeLeft / ViewShakeDuration, 0.f, 1.f);
484: 	const float Omega = ViewShakeFrequency * 2.f * UE_PI;
485: 	const float Pitch = ViewShakeAmplitudeDeg * Fade * FMath::Sin(Omega * Elapsed);
486: 	const float Yaw = ViewShakeAmplitudeDeg * Fade * FMath::Sin(Omega * 1.13f * Elapsed + 1.3f);
487: 	Camera->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator(Pitch, Yaw, 0.f));
488: }

823: 		Controller->SetControlRotation(Snapshot.ControlRotation);

825: 	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))

831: 	{
832: 		StatusEffects->RemoveAllEffects();
833: 	}

836: 	const float RespawnHealth = RespawnData ? RespawnData->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::RespawnHealthFraction, 1.f) : 1.f;
837: 	const float RespawnMana = RespawnData ? RespawnData->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::RespawnManaFraction, 1.f) : 1.f;
838: 	const bool bRespawnRefill = RespawnData ? RespawnData->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::RespawnRefillAmmo, 1.f) > 0.5f : true;
839: 	RestoreAll(RespawnHealth, RespawnMana, bRespawnRefill, /*bOnlyRaise*/ false);

// Names of the stats used in the JSON data files and in the modifier system.
// Every number that tunes gameplay is a named stat: base value from data, then modifiers
// (skill tree, armor, status effects, air passives, weapon enchant) through UGBStatsComponent.
// Use these constants instead of typing strings, so a typo is a compile error.
// Adding a stat: add the constant here, read it where it is used, and put its value in the JSON.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"

namespace GBStat
{
	// ---- Actor stats (Player.json "Stats", Enemies.json "Stats") ----
	inline const FName MaxHealth(TEXT("MaxHealth"));
	inline const FName HealthRegen(TEXT("HealthRegen"));            // per second
	inline const FName HealthRegenDelay(TEXT("HealthRegenDelay"));  // seconds after taking damage
	inline const FName MaxMana(TEXT("MaxMana"));
	inline const FName ManaRegen(TEXT("ManaRegen"));                // per second
	inline const FName ManaRegenDelay(TEXT("ManaRegenDelay"));      // seconds after spending mana
	inline const FName MoveSpeed(TEXT("MoveSpeed"));                // cm/s
	inline const FName JumpVelocity(TEXT("JumpVelocity"));          // cm/s
	inline const FName MaxJumps(TEXT("MaxJumps"));                  // 1 = single jump, 2 = double jump
	inline const FName AirControl(TEXT("AirControl"));              // 0 = jumps commit to their direction (GDD 18)
	inline const FName JumpTakeoffMinSpeedMult(TEXT("JumpTakeoffMinSpeedMult")); // min takeoff speed as a fraction of MoveSpeed (ground jump)
	inline const FName AirJumpMinSpeedMult(TEXT("AirJumpMinSpeedMult"));         // min takeoff speed as a fraction of MoveSpeed (air jump)
	inline const FName GravityScale(TEXT("GravityScale"));
	inline const FName DashDistance(TEXT("DashDistance"));          // cm
	inline const FName DashDuration(TEXT("DashDuration"));          // seconds
	inline const FName DashCooldown(TEXT("DashCooldown"));          // seconds
	inline const FName InteractRange(TEXT("InteractRange"));        // cm
	inline const FName DamageTaken(TEXT("DamageTaken"));            // multiplier, base 1
	inline const FName DamageDealt(TEXT("DamageDealt"));            // multiplier, base 1
	inline const FName CapsuleRadius(TEXT("CapsuleRadius"));
	inline const FName CapsuleHalfHeight(TEXT("CapsuleHalfHeight"));
	inline const FName EyeHeight(TEXT("EyeHeight"));
	inline const FName AccelerationPerSpeed(TEXT("AccelerationPerSpeed")); // walk-speed multiples per second (snappy movement)
	inline const FName BrakingPerSpeed(TEXT("BrakingPerSpeed"));
	inline const FName GroundFriction(TEXT("GroundFriction"));
	inline const FName InteractFacingMinDot(TEXT("InteractFacingMinDot")); // interactables must be this much in front of the view
	inline const FName LookSensitivity(TEXT("LookSensitivity"));           // mouse degrees per input unit
	inline const FName StickYawRate(TEXT("StickYawRate"));                // gamepad look degrees per second at full tilt
	inline const FName StickPitchRate(TEXT("StickPitchRate"));
	inline const FName PitchLimit(TEXT("PitchLimit"));                    // max look pitch, degrees
	inline const FName DashExitSpeedMult(TEXT("DashExitSpeedMult"));       // speed kept after a dash, as a fraction of walk speed
	inline const FName PrecisionRecovery(TEXT("PrecisionRecovery")); // owned rank of the weapon perk (skill modifier only, base 0)

	// ---- Enemy stats (Enemies.json "Stats") ----
	inline const FName AttackDamage(TEXT("AttackDamage"));
	inline const FName AttackRange(TEXT("AttackRange"));
	inline const FName AttackInterval(TEXT("AttackInterval"));
	inline const FName AttackWindup(TEXT("AttackWindup"));
	inline const FName AggroRange(TEXT("AggroRange"));
	inline const FName HeadFraction(TEXT("HeadFraction"));          // top fraction of the body that counts as head (GDD 3)

	// ---- Weapon stats (Weapons.json "Stats"); modifier keys are Weapon.<Id>.<Stat> and Weapon.All.<Stat> ----
	inline const FName Damage(TEXT("Damage"));
	inline const FName FireInterval(TEXT("FireInterval"));          // seconds between shots
	inline const FName MagazineSize(TEXT("MagazineSize"));
	inline const FName SpareMagazines(TEXT("SpareMagazines"));      // spare magazines after a full refill
	inline const FName ReloadTime(TEXT("ReloadTime"));
	inline const FName ReloadPerRound(TEXT("ReloadPerRound"));      // 1 = loads shell by shell, SpareMagazines then counts rounds
	inline const FName Range(TEXT("Range"));
	inline const FName Spread(TEXT("Spread"));                      // degrees, hip fire
	inline const FName AimSpread(TEXT("AimSpread"));                // degrees, aiming
	inline const FName MoveSpread(TEXT("MoveSpread"));              // extra degrees at full speed, hip fire
	inline const FName AimMoveSpread(TEXT("AimMoveSpread"));        // extra degrees at full speed, aiming
	inline const FName RecoilPitch(TEXT("RecoilPitch"));            // degrees per shot
	inline const FName RecoilYaw(TEXT("RecoilYaw"));                // +- degrees per shot
	inline const FName RecoilRecovery(TEXT("RecoilRecovery"));      // degrees per second
	inline const FName Pellets(TEXT("Pellets"));
	inline const FName FalloffStart(TEXT("FalloffStart"));          // cm: up to here, FalloffNearMult
	inline const FName FalloffEnd(TEXT("FalloffEnd"));              // cm: from here on, FalloffFarMult
	inline const FName FalloffNearMult(TEXT("FalloffNearMult"));
	inline const FName FalloffFarMult(TEXT("FalloffFarMult"));
	inline const FName HeadshotMult(TEXT("HeadshotMult"));
	inline const FName ProjectileSpeed(TEXT("ProjectileSpeed"));
	inline const FName ProjectileRadius(TEXT("ProjectileRadius"));
	inline const FName ProjectileGravity(TEXT("ProjectileGravity"));
	inline const FName AimFOV(TEXT("AimFOV"));
	inline const FName AimMoveSpeedMult(TEXT("AimMoveSpeedMult"));
	inline const FName MeleeRange(TEXT("MeleeRange"));
	inline const FName MeleeArc(TEXT("MeleeArc"));                  // degrees

	// ---- Spell parameters (Spells.json "Params"); modifier keys are Spell.<Id>.<Param> and Spell.All.<Param> ----
	inline const FName ManaCost(TEXT("ManaCost"));
	inline const FName Cooldown(TEXT("Cooldown"));
	inline const FName Speed(TEXT("Speed"));
	inline const FName Radius(TEXT("Radius"));
	inline const FName Duration(TEXT("Duration"));
	inline const FName Width(TEXT("Width"));
	inline const FName Height(TEXT("Height"));
	inline const FName Thickness(TEXT("Thickness"));
	inline const FName Health(TEXT("Health"));
	inline const FName TickInterval(TEXT("TickInterval"));
	inline const FName DamagePerSecond(TEXT("DamagePerSecond"));
	inline const FName EffectDuration(TEXT("EffectDuration"));      // multiplier on the on-hit status effect duration
	inline const FName EffectStrength(TEXT("EffectStrength"));      // multiplier on the on-hit status effect strength
	inline const FName BlockFraction(TEXT("BlockFraction"));        // ice shield: 0..1 of frontal damage removed
	inline const FName FuseTime(TEXT("FuseTime"));

	// ---- QTE (QTE.json "Stats"); also modifiable by gear (GDD 7) ----
	inline const FName QTETimeDilation(TEXT("QTE.TimeDilation"));   // world speed during the QTE (0.1 = 90% slow)
	inline const FName QTETimeLimit(TEXT("QTE.TimeLimit"));         // real seconds for the whole QTE
	inline const FName QTESequenceLength(TEXT("QTE.SequenceLength"));
	inline const FName QTEGestureCount(TEXT("QTE.GestureCount"));

	// ---- Elemental build-up (GDD 6): spell param / item stat, weapon stat ----
	inline const FName BuildUp(TEXT("BuildUp"));                   // raw build-up per hit or per tick
	inline const FName WeakPointBuildUpMult(TEXT("WeakPointBuildUpMult")); // weapon stat; multiplies BuildUp on weak point hits
	inline const FName EnchantBuildUpMult(TEXT("EnchantBuildUpMult"));     // weapon stat; multiplies BuildUp when the hit's element comes from the weapon enchant

	/** "Weapon.<WeaponId>.<Stat>" */
	inline FName WeaponKey(FName WeaponId, FName Stat) { return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Weapon.%s.%s"), *WeaponId.ToString(), *Stat.ToString())); }
	/** "Weapon.Staff.<Stat>", "Weapon.Gun.<Stat>" or "Weapon.Sword.<Stat>" */
	inline FName WeaponSlotKey(EGBHeldSlot Slot, FName Stat)
	{
		const TCHAR* SlotName = TEXT("Gun");
		switch (Slot)
		{
		case EGBHeldSlot::Staff: SlotName = TEXT("Staff"); break;
		case EGBHeldSlot::Gun:   SlotName = TEXT("Gun"); break;
		case EGBHeldSlot::Sword:  SlotName = TEXT("Sword"); break;
		}
		return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Weapon.%s.%s"), SlotName, *Stat.ToString()));
	}
	/** "Weapon.All.<Stat>" */
	inline FName WeaponAllKey(FName Stat) { return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Weapon.All.%s"), *Stat.ToString())); }
	/** "Spell.<SpellId>.<Param>" */
	inline FName SpellKey(FName SpellId, FName Param) { return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Spell.%s.%s"), *SpellId.ToString(), *Param.ToString())); }
	/** "Spell.All.<Param>" */
	inline FName SpellAllKey(FName Param) { return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Spell.All.%s"), *Param.ToString())); }
}

	inline const FName CooldownPerRank(TEXT("PrecisionRecoveryCooldownPerRank")); // seconds less per rank
	inline const FName CooldownMin(TEXT("PrecisionRecoveryCooldownMin"));        // lowest possible cooldown

#include "Progression/GBSkillTreeComponent.h"

#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

UGBSkillTreeComponent::UGBSkillTreeComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		DataReloadedHandle = Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &UGBSkillTreeComponent::HandleDataReloaded);
	}
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.Remove(DataReloadedHandle);
	}
	DataReloadedHandle.Reset();

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::InitializeFromData()
{
	RemoveAllModifiers();
	Ranks.Reset();
	PointsSpent = 0;

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Skill tree: no data subsystem, starting with 0 points."));
	}
	Points = Data ? FMath::Max(0, Data->GetPlayer().StartingSkillPoints) : 0;
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::AddPoints(int32 Amount)
{
	if (Amount == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	Points = FMath::Max(0, Points + Amount);
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
}

int32 UGBSkillTreeComponent::GetRank(FName NodeId) const
{
	return Ranks.FindRef(NodeId);
}

EGBSkillUnlockResult UGBSkillTreeComponent::CanUnlock(FName NodeId) const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Node = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
	if (!Node)
	{
		return EGBSkillUnlockResult::UnknownNode;
	}
	return EvaluateUnlock(*Node, Ranks, Points);
}

EGBSkillUnlockResult UGBSkillTreeComponent::Unlock(FName NodeId)
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Node = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
	if (!Node)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Unlock: unknown skill node '%s'."), *NodeId.ToString());
		return EGBSkillUnlockResult::UnknownNode;
	}

	const EGBSkillUnlockResult Result = EvaluateUnlock(*Node, Ranks, Points);
	if (Result != EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok)
	{
		return Result;
	}

	Points -= FMath::Max(0, Node->Cost);
	PointsSpent += FMath::Max(0, Node->Cost);
	Ranks.FindOrAdd(NodeId) += 1;
	ApplyNodeModifiers(NodeId);
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
	return EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok;
}

int32 UGBSkillTreeComponent::ResetAll()
{
	if (Ranks.Num() == 0)
	{
		return 0;
	}
	RemoveAllModifiers();
	const int32 Refunded = PointsSpent;
	Points += PointsSpent;
	PointsSpent = 0;
	Ranks.Reset();
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
	return Refunded;
}

EGBSkillUnlockResult UGBSkillTreeComponent::EvaluateUnlock(const FGBSkillNodeDefinition& Node, const TMap<FName, int32>& InRanks, int32 AvailablePoints)
{
	if (InRanks.FindRef(Node.Id) >= Node.MaxRank)
	{
		return EGBSkillUnlockResult::MaxRank;
	}
	for (const FName& Required : Node.Requires)
	{
		if (!Required.IsNone() && InRanks.FindRef(Required) <= 0)
		{
			return EGBSkillUnlockResult::MissingRequirement;
		}
	}
	if (AvailablePoints < Node.Cost)
	{
		return EGBSkillUnlockResult::NotEnoughPoints;
	}
	return EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok;
}

FName UGBSkillTreeComponent::ModifierSource(FName NodeId)
{
	return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Skill.%s"), *NodeId.ToString()));
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::ApplyNodeModifiers(FName NodeId)
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FName Source = ModifierSource(NodeId);
	const int32 Rank = GetRank(NodeId);
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Node = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
	if (!Node || Rank <= 0)
	{
		if (!Node && Rank > 0)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Skill node '%s' is owned but missing from Skills.json; its modifiers are off."), *NodeId.ToString());
		}
		Stats->RemoveModifiers(Source);
		return;
	}

	// ValueScale = rank: Add modifiers count once per rank, Multiply modifiers stack linearly (not compounding).
	Stats->AddModifiers(Source, Node->ModifiersPerRank, static_cast<float>(Rank));
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::RemoveAllModifiers()
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return;
	}
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Ranks)
	{
		Stats->RemoveModifiers(ModifierSource(Pair.Key));
	}
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::ReapplyAllModifiers()
{
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Ranks)
	{
		ApplyNodeModifiers(Pair.Key);
	}
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.SkillPoints = Points;
	Snapshot.SkillPointsSpent = PointsSpent;
	Snapshot.SkillRanks = Ranks;
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	RemoveAllModifiers();

	Points = FMath::Max(0, Snapshot.SkillPoints);
	PointsSpent = FMath::Max(0, Snapshot.SkillPointsSpent);
	Ranks.Reset();
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Snapshot.SkillRanks)
	{
		if (Pair.Value > 0)
		{
			Ranks.Add(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
		}
	}

	ReapplyAllModifiers();
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::HandleDataReloaded()
{
	ReapplyAllModifiers();
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool AGBPickup::GiveDrop(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, FGBDropDefinition& InOutDrop, bool& bOutAllTaken)
{
	bOutAllTaken = false;
	if (!Player)
	{
		return false;
	}

	if (!InOutDrop.ItemId.IsNone())
	{
		UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player->GetInventory();
		if (!Inventory)
		{
			return false;
		}
		const int32 Wanted = FMath::Max(1, InOutDrop.Count);
		const int32 Taken = Inventory->AddItem(InOutDrop.ItemId, Wanted);
		if (Taken <= 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		InOutDrop.Count = Wanted - Taken;
		bOutAllTaken = InOutDrop.Count <= 0;
		return true;
	}

	if (!InOutDrop.WeaponId.IsNone())
	{
		// Owner's rule (2026-11-12): a weapon never replaces the one in hand and nothing is left on the floor.
		// A new weapon goes to its slot if that is empty, otherwise it is stored (equipped later from the inventory).
		UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(Player);
		const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId) : nullptr;
		if (!Equipment || !Def)
		{
			return false;
		}
		const bool bGun = Def->Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun;
		if (Equipment->OwnsWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId))
		{
			// A copy of a gun already owned is worth spare magazines if that gun is in the slot; otherwise it is just used up.
			if (bGun && Equipment->IsWeaponEquipped(InOutDrop.WeaponId))
			{
				const UGBDataSubsystem* GameData = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(Player);
				const int32 Magazines = GameData ? FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GameData->GetGameValue(GBPickupKey::DuplicateGunMagazines, 1.f))) : 1;
				Equipment->AddSpareMagazines(Magazines);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			Equipment->GiveWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId, true);
			if (!Equipment->OwnsWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId))
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		bOutAllTaken = true;
		return true;
	}

	if (!InOutDrop.SpellGroup.IsNone())
	{
		UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook();
{

=== Casting ===
Mana 100, +6/s after 1.5 s without spending. Average direct spell: 15 mana, 61.7 damage at a typical QTE.
Sustained: one cast every 4 game s; with the starting bar over a 75 s fight, about 20.9 damage/s on one target (37.6 in a wave).
Each QTE adds about 1.8 real seconds on top of the game time.

void AGBPlayerCharacter::ReleaseHeldInputs()
{
	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment ? Equipment->GetActiveItem() : nullptr)
	{
		Item->StopPrimary();
		Item->StopSecondary();
	}
	if (Spellbook)
	{
		Spellbook->SetBlockHeld(false);
	}
	bSecondaryHeld = false;
	LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	StopJumping();
}

{
	bControlEnabled = bEnabled;
	if (!bEnabled)
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/GameInstance.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"

UGBProgressSubsystem* UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (const UGameInstance* DirectInstance = Cast<UGameInstance>(WorldContextObject))
	{
		return DirectInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBProgressSubsystem>();
	}
	const UWorld* World = GEngine ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = World ? World->GetGameInstance() : nullptr;
	return GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBProgressSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

bool UGBProgressSubsystem::HasFlag(FName Flag) const
{
	return Flags.Contains(Flag);
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::SetFlag(FName Flag, bool bValue)
{
	if (Flag.IsNone())
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Progress: SetFlag called with an empty flag name."));
		return;
	}

	const bool bChanged = bValue ? !Flags.Contains(Flag) : Flags.Contains(Flag);
	if (!bChanged)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (bValue)
	{
		Flags.Add(Flag);
	}
	else
	{
		Flags.Remove(Flag);
	}
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Log, TEXT("Progress: flag %s = %s"), *Flag.ToString(), bValue ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false"));
	OnFlagChanged.Broadcast(Flag);
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::SaveCheckpoint(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!Player)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Progress: SaveCheckpoint without a player."));
		return;
	}

	FGBPlayerSnapshot Snapshot;
	Player->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Snapshot.bValid = true;
	Checkpoint = MoveTemp(Snapshot);
	OnCheckpointSaved.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBProgressSubsystem::RestoreCheckpoint(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!Player || !Checkpoint.bValid)
	{
		return false;
	}
	Player->RespawnAt(Checkpoint);
	return true;
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::ResetProgress()
{
	const TSet<FName> Cleared = MoveTemp(Flags);
	Flags.Reset();
	Checkpoint = FGBPlayerSnapshot();
	for (const FName& Flag : Cleared)
	{
		OnFlagChanged.Broadcast(Flag);
	}
}

codex
Found three issues introduced by making magazine capacity change at runtime.

1. **[P1] Reset leaves live guns over capacity; swapping guns then deletes ammo.**  
   [GBGun.cpp:1209](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBGun.cpp:1209), [GBGun.cpp:1225](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBGun.cpp:1225)  
   Capacity reads current perks, but existing ammo is only clamped by `SetAmmo`. Fill an Extended Tube rank-3 shotgun to **9 loaded / 36 reserve**, then reset skills: it retains **9 / 36**, although its limits are now **6 / 24**. The HUD shows nine rounds alongside six loaded pips and `36 SHELLS`; all excess rounds remain usable. Equip another gun, then re-equip the shotgun: restoration clamps it to **6 / 24**, silently deleting fifteen shells. Extended Mag similarly leaves a reset rifle holding 45 rounds until restoration clamps it to 30.  
   **Fix:** reconcile live and stored ammo consistently when skill changes finish, using one capacity-change policy. Apply the same policy during restoration so swapping weapons cannot independently change the outcome.

2. **[P2] Changing tube capacity mid-reload leaves timing and progress based on the old capacity.**  
   [GBGun.cpp:275](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBGun.cpp:275), [GBGun.cpp:1449](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBGun.cpp:1449)  
   `Begin` captures `CycleSeconds` and `Planned`, but `Advance` receives the current capacity. Start reloading an empty six-shell shotgun, open skills before the first shell loads, buy three Extended Tube ranks, then resume. It still loads at **2/6 seconds per shell**, now continuing to nine shells: roughly **three seconds**, rather than two. After six shells, the HUD progress bar reaches 100% while the gun continues loading three more. Resetting during a nine-shell reload produces the inverse timing mismatch.  
   **Fix:** cancel and restart the remaining reload when capacity changes, or recompute cycle duration and planned progress together. The gameplay and progress display must use consistent capacity state. I found no infinite-loop or over-capacity insertion in `Advance` itself.

3. **[P2] The gun-mounted pip display stays stale after purchasing magazine perks.**  
   [GBGun.cpp:1088](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBGun.cpp:1088)  
   The render target updates only when the displayed ammo text changes, although pip geometry also depends on capacity. With six shells loaded, buy one Extended Tube rank: the main HUD shows seven positions, but the gun screen retains its old six lit pips until firing or loading changes the count. Extended Mag produces the same stale-capacity display on the rifle.  
   **Fix:** include capacity in the display cache/invalidation condition.

The balance script runs successfully, and data validation confirms **30 unique, reachable nodes with no missing prerequisites**. Quick Draw reaches **1.0 movement multiplier** and **0.06-second aim time** at rank three; movement accepts that multiplier, and aim interpolation guards near-zero division. Normal checkpoint respawn refills using current perks rather than restoring snapshot ammo.

Read-only review; no files changed. Runtime sequences above are derived from source, not an Unreal playtest.
